- 1616 files · ~2,338,506 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 34403 nodes · 70553 edges · 1362 communities (1172 shown, 190 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6895 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `98dfeee6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
